## 📝 Duplicate Page Titles

Pages should have a descriptive, unique `<title>` so users can tell them apart in browser tabs, history, and bookmarks ([WCAG 2.4.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/page-titled.html)).

- **"Just a moment..."** — 10 pages:
  - https://www.unrwa.org/

## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 11 page(s) - 40 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R73: Paragraphs of text have a line height that is not too small](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73)
**Issue**: The line height of the paragraph is less than 1.5

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<p>...</p>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/p
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/p')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.unrwa.org/
- https://donate.unrwa.org/int/en/gaza
- https://www.unfpa.org/donate#palestine
- https://www.msf.org/palestine
- https://internationalmedicalcorps.org/country/gaza/
- *...and 6 more page(s)*

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://www.moh.gov.ps/portal/pharmaceutical-statistics/

#### Rule: [SIA-R11: Link elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r11)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.4.4](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/link-purpose-in-context), [SC 2.4.9](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/), [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-13
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="https://twitter.com/MOH_PR" class="social-icon">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="social-icon"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-13
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.moh.masar" class="twitter">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="twitter"]`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-13
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="https://apps.apple.com/us/app/masaar-مسار/id6764005947">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-13
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="http://www.moh.gov.ps/portal/pharmaceutical-statistics">إحصائيات دوائية</a>`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-11-13
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.moh.masar" class="twitter">...</a>`

#### Rule: [SIA-R2: Images have an accessible name or are marked as decorative](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r2)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.1.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/non-text-content) (Level A)

- XPath: `/img[@class="img-app"]`

- XPath: `/h5[@class="text-uppercase fusion-responsive-typography-calculated"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R66: Text has enhanced contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AAA)
